# Graph Report - Moriarty  (2026-09-11)

## Corpus Check
- Large corpus: 42275 files · ~46,277,977 words. Semantic extraction will be expensive (many Claude tokens). Consider running on a subfolder.

## Summary
- 15085 nodes · 29827 edges · 1205 communities (816 shown, 323 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1236 edges (avg confidence: 0.86)
- Terminal measured usage: 5,832,203 input · 30,003,840 cache-read input · 1,754,804 output; reported cost $6.60580934 (main Grok invocation; see ../grok-measured-usage.json). Per-fragment zero fields were placeholders, not measured usage.
